## 8. EXTENDED THEORETICAL IMPLICATIONS

Sub-chapter 8.1: What does it mean to be human when your neural net is distributed across biological tissue and silicon servers? The BCGN nanobots create a seamless hive-mind architecture. The individual 'I' dissolves into a 'We', yet retains the qualitative experience (Qualia) of individuality through the Homeostatic Illusion Matrix (HIM). The future of this system is not just biological enhancement, but the conquest of mortality itself.
